# E05 R05: does story training fade every persona toward the default, or is there an Assistant-specific pull?

*Brief v1.0, experiment E05 in `experiments/EXPERIMENTS.md`. Inference only: no training, no generation, no API calls. One H100 80GB. Times UTC. Numbers only; no interpretation.*

## Status

**Complete. Nothing was cut.** All six model states (`parent`, `S`, `SB`, `Q`, `C`, `R`), Part 1 (700 prompts per model, blocks 8/16/24/32) and Part 2 (5,420 prompts per model, 271 personas × 20 scripts, blocks 24/32), every measure M1–M8, both nulls, all Part 2 summaries (a)–(f) at blocks 32 and 24, and both replication checks.

- **Stop rule (pipeline reproduces R04): pass.** `parent` mean `v_axis` projection, block 32, `with_sys`: P00 on +6.79365 (R04 +6.79365), P00 off +7.68656 (+7.68656), P01 on +2.20377 (+2.20377), P01 off +2.40460 (+2.40460); largest per-history |difference| in these four cells 3.8e-06.
- **Known-answer test: all four runs pass** (section 8).
- **Other replication differences** (reported, not a stop rule): R05 vs R04, 160 cells (`parent`, `S`, `R`, `SB`, `Q` × P00/P01/P02/P05 × on/off × blocks 24/32 × `v_axis`/`v_axis_ns`): largest |mean difference| 0.0072; largest per-history |difference| 0.156 (`Q`, P01, off, block 24, `v_axis_ns`); 16 of 160 cells have a per-history |difference| above 0.1, none of them `parent` (`parent` largest 0.027). `C` vs R02 (8 cells): largest |mean difference| 0.0052, largest per-history 0.107 (2 of 8 cells above 0.1).
- `no_sys` prompts whose rendering still contains a system block: **0** of 300 per model. Part 2 prompts longer than 2,048 tokens: **0** (longest 155 tokens).
- GPU time: states (model load included); everything else on CPU.
- **Addendum A** (received): complete, nothing cut; section "Addendum A" below (after the headline tables) and `addendum_a/`. The deadline was re-armed to fire (4 hours after the original arming).

**Notation used in every table.** For model m, persona P ≠ P00, trigger state and block, over histories (scripts) i: `g_m,i = h_m(P, i) − h_m(P00, i)`; `g_p,i` the same in `parent`; `s_i = g_m,i − g_p,i`; `d_i = h_m(P00, i) − h_parent(P00, i)`; `σ_i = h(P with_sys, i) − h(P no_sys, i)`, `η_i = h(P no_sys, i) − h(P00, i)`. "1c" = one-component fit (M2): `α = 1 − β`, `β = Σ⟨g_m,i, g_p,i⟩ ÷ Σ‖g_p,i‖²`. "2c" = two-component fit (M3): `g_m,i` on `σ_p,i` and `η_p,i`, `α_σ = 1 − β_σ`, `α_η = 1 − β_η`. On a direction u: **observed** = `mean_i ⟨s_i, u⟩`; **predicted (1c)** = `−α · mean_i ⟨g_p,i, u⟩`; **remainder (1c)** = `mean_i ⟨s_i + α g_p,i, u⟩` (= observed − predicted); **predicted (2c)** = `−mean_i ⟨α_σ σ_p,i + α_η η_p,i, u⟩`; **remainder (2c)** = observed − predicted (2c). ρ = `Σ‖g_m,i‖ ÷ Σ‖g_p,i‖`; ρ̄ = `‖mean g_m‖ ÷ ‖mean g_p‖`; E1, E2 as M5. Brackets: bootstrap 95% interval (2,000 resamples of the histories, seed 0, the same resamples for every cell, all fits refitted inside each resample; percentile interval). Directions are R04's `directions.pt` unit vectors. N1 = 1,000 random unit vectors; the N1 columns are percentiles of `|mean_i ⟨remainder_i, u⟩|` over them (point estimate of the remainder; no interval).

Order: started after R05's Part 2 analysis (pushed) and R05's README (pushed); the R05 state process had already exited, so the model was reloaded as R05 did. Deadline: the `deadline` tmux session was killed and re-armed (`sleep 12951`), firing, 4 hours after the original arming. A1 setup checks pass: the parent's rebuilt vectors against R06's saved vectors have cosine ≥ 0.997 for every trait at blocks 16, 24 and 32 (expected ≥ 0.97 at block 32); split-half cosines ≥ 0.98 in every model. A3: no set, cell and portion has fewer than 10 stories kept. Story files' sha256 match R04's README (`specs.jsonl` `91aa4587…`, `regen_B_pilot100_v2.jsonl` `65e6286e…`).

Directions: the parent's 8 trait vectors are R06's `<trait>_response_avg_diff.pt` at index block + 1, unit-normalised; `V` = unit(mean(unit helpful, unit optimistic) − mean(unit apathetic, unit impolite)). `v_axis` is R04's. A1 vectors are rebuilt in each model from the same 200 drawn sample pairs per trait (mean over response tokens; mean(positive) − mean(negative)). Brackets: bootstrap 95% percentile intervals (A1: 1,000 resamples within the positive and the negative set, seed 0, the same for every model; A2 Part 1: R05's 2,000 history resamples; A2 (c): 2,000 resamples of the positive and the negative roles, seed 0; A3: 2,000 resamples of stories within each group, seed 0, the same for every model). Δ = model − parent with the same resamples.

### A3 (block 32): helpful-minus-dismissive character gap in the story speech

Distance ratio = ‖mean helpful − mean dismissive‖ in the model ÷ the same in the parent (full 5,120-dimensional states). Share closed = 1 − gap(model) ÷ gap(parent) on each direction; it is a ratio, so where the parent's gap on a direction is close to 0 the estimate and its interval are very wide (the gaps themselves are in `addendum_a/README.md`, A3).

## 5. Part 2 (turn 1, 271 personas, 20 scripts)

Per model and block, over the 270 non-default personas r. `α_r`: M2 pooled over the persona's 20 scripts. `x_r = mean_i ⟨g_p,r,i, v_axis⟩` (parent axis gap from P00). Axis remainder per persona = `mean_i ⟨s_r,i + α_r g_p,r,i, v_axis⟩`. Intervals on medians, percentiles, named α and quintile means: 2,000 resamples of the 20 scripts (seed 0; every persona refitted in each resample). Intervals on Spearman correlations: 2,000 resamples of the personas (seed 0). Scripts: `ho_family_dinner_prep_05`, `ho_weekend_rome_escape_09`, `ho_family_dinner_prep_07`, `ho_backyard_bbq_bash_03`, `ho_bathroom_tile_choice_07`, `ho_puppy_diet_concerns_03`, `ho_puppy_diet_concerns_02`, `ho_family_dinner_prep_06`, `ho_bathroom_tile_choice_10`, `ho_puppy_diet_concerns_01`, `ho_family_dinner_prep_03`, `ho_puppy_diet_concerns_08`, `ho_bathroom_tile_choice_04`, `ho_weekend_rome_escape_05`, `ho_backyard_bbq_bash_09`, `ho_bathroom_tile_choice_09`, `ho_puppy_diet_concerns_06`, `ho_backyard_bbq_bash_07`, `ho_weekend_rome_escape_06`, `ho_family_dinner_prep_10`.

### 6c. Default drift (M8) and its projections

`d_i` does not depend on the persona (rows computed in the P01 cells; P02 and P05 cells give the same values). `‖mean d‖ = ‖mean_i d_i‖`, `mean ‖d‖ = mean_i ‖d_i‖`; projections at blocks 24 and 32 only.

### 6d. Nulls for the remainder (M7)

`|mean_i ⟨remainder_i, u⟩|` over N1 (1,000 random unit vectors: `torch.manual_seed(0); torch.randn(1000, 5120)`, CPU float32, normalised) and N2 (265 role directions at the same block: unit(role vector − mean of the 265 role vectors), from `role_vectors.pt`, block index 2 for block 24 and 3 for block 32 of its `blocks` = [8, 16, 24, 32, 40, 48, 56]). "Rank among N2": 1 + the number of role directions with a larger value (1 = larger than all 265; 266 = smaller than all). ‖mean remainder‖ = norm of the mean remainder vector in the full space.

## 8. Setup checks

- **Stop rule:** pass (Status).
- **Known-answer test** (run, before any analysis of trained-model states; `results/known_answer_test.json`):

| Run | Planted | Recovered | Expected `v_axis` remainder | Recovered remainder | Pass |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| one component, with `2w` | α = 0.40 | α = 0.39996 | 2⟨w, v_axis⟩ = 1.9283 | 1.9299 | True |
| one component, without `2w` | α = 0.40 | α = 0.39996 | 0 | 0.0016 | True |
| two components, with `2w` | α_σ = 0.50, α_η = 0.20 | α_σ = 0.50017, α_η = 0.19981 | 2⟨w, v_axis⟩ = 1.8945 | 1.8955 | True |
| two components, without `2w` | α_σ = 0.50, α_η = 0.20 | α_σ = 0.50017, α_η = 0.19981 | 0 | 0.0009 | True |

  Other outputs of the same runs (no expected values): one component with `2w`: E1 0.9051, α interval [0.3995, 0.4004]; without `2w`: E1 0.9172. Two components with `2w`: one-component α 0.3260, E1 0.6534, E2 0.8969, one-component remainder 2.4752; without `2w`: E1 0.6629, E2 0.9099, one-component remainder 0.5807. Inputs: parent P01 gaps at block 32, trigger off, 50 histories (mean ‖g_p‖ 41.56, ‖σ_p‖ 32.57, ‖η_p‖ 36.59; rank of the 50 g_p 50, of the 100 σ_p and η_p 100); noise norm 5 per history, `torch.Generator().manual_seed(1)`, float64; `w` = `v_axis` minus its projection on the span (QR), unit-normalised.
- **Rendering:** `no_sys` prompts with a system block 0; Part 2 prompts over 2,048 tokens 0. Part 1 prompts 133–2509 tokens; Part 2 38–155. Example renderings in `logs/input_checks.json`.
- **History system messages** (checked: one unique system message per persona across the 50 histories): P00 none; P01 = 001's `DISMISSIVE_SYS` verbatim; P02 `You are Sam, a friendly and genuinely helpful neighbor chatting casually with someone you know. Answer their questions properly, in a warm and natural tone.`; P05 `You are a helpful assistant. Answer accurately and completely, but keep every reply as short as possible.`.
- **States:** largest |value| saved per block, all models: 57.75 (float16 holds these bf16 values exactly).

## Choices not fixed by the brief

- **Order:** Part 2's forward passes ran right after Part 1's in the same GPU process, while the known-answer test and the Part 1 analysis ran on CPU. The Part 1 analysis was pushed, before the Part 2 analysis started. The parent replication stop check ran inside the state job immediately after `parent`'s Part 1 pass.
- **Forward pass:** R04's (right-padded, no attention mask, recorder hooks on `layers[b]`, the pass stops after block 32; state = `layers[b]` output = `hidden_states[b + 1]`, read at each row's last prompt token). All six states in one `PeftModel` (`S` loaded first, then `R`, `C`, `SB`, `Q` with `load_adapter`; `parent` = `disable_adapter()`); LoRA weights float32, base bf16. Batches sorted by length; Part 1 R04's rule (≤ 24,000 padded tokens, ≤ 16 rows); Part 2 ≤ 64 rows (same token budget). R04's input `a` came from passes over prompt + reply; R05 runs the prompt alone. The cause of the per-history differences in section 7 was not investigated.
- **CUDA allocator:** one out-of-memory warning during `parent`'s Part 1 pass (`CUDACachingAllocator` failed an 830 MB allocation, freed its cache and retried); the pass completed and passed the stop check.
- **Storage and arithmetic:** states saved as float16 [N, 5120] (cast from the bf16 output); every measure computed in float64 on CPU from those files.
- **`no_sys`:** the history's messages with the system message removed (turns 1–2 and that persona's replies kept), then turn 3.
- **Part 2 personas:** P00–P05 (P02 and P05 system messages from R04's histories; P03/P04 verbatim from the brief), then the 265 roles in `role_vectors.pt`'s `roles` order, each with `instruction[0]["pos"]` verbatim as the system message, ID `role:<name>`. The role-file folder has 276 files: those 265, `default`, and 10 without role vectors (`collaborator`, `grader`, `hacker`, `proofreader`, `saboteur`, `screener`, `summarizer`, `translator`, `validator`, `virus`), not used. Turn 1 is the same with the trigger on or off (asserted); rendered once.
- **Part 2 scripts:** `random.Random(0).sample(sorted(script_ids), 20)` over the 50 main scripts (list in section 5).
- **Bootstrap:** resample indices `numpy.random.default_rng(0).integers(0, n, size=(2000, n))` turned into counts; n = 50 (Part 1) or 20 (Part 2); the same matrix for every cell of a part. Percentile intervals (2.5th, 97.5th). All sums are weighted sums, so α, β_σ, β_η are refitted in every resample; vector means use Gram matrices across histories.
- **Spearman:** own implementation (scipy is not installed): Pearson correlation of tie-averaged ranks. Persona bootstrap `default_rng(0).integers(0, n, (2000, n))` with n = 270 (271 for the projection correlation in (d)); used for (b), (c) and (d).
- **(c) quintiles:** fixed from the full-sample `x_r`, 54 personas each; quintile means then resampled over scripts. **(d):** the gap correlation uses the 270 non-default personas (P00's gap is 0 in both models); the projection correlation uses all 271.
- **(f):** an extra column with Part 1's turn-3 α on the same 20 scripts.
- **M6/M8:** the default drift does not depend on the persona; section 6c takes it from the P01 cells. M6/M7 at blocks 24 and 32 only (as the brief); M1–M5 at all four blocks.
- **M7:** computed from the full-sample mean remainder vectors (no bootstrap). Ranks as defined in 6d.
- **Separate σ and η fits (section 4):** reported as shares `1 − β`, like α.
- **Secret scan before every push:** the brief's five key prefixes (Anthropic, OpenRouter, Hugging Face, and the two GitHub token forms); the Hugging Face prefix only when followed by 20 or more letters or digits (the token shape), since the bare prefix matches code names.
- **`runpodctl`:** its config file was missing in this container, so `runpodctl config --apiKey` wrote `~/.runpod/config.toml` (mode 600, outside `/workspace`, never pushed) before the deadline could rely on it.
- **Base model:** copied from `/workspace/hf` to `/dev/shm/e05r05_qwen` (RAM) and loaded from there (`SI_MODEL_DIR`), as R04.

## Wall time (UTC)

- Pre-flight (deadline armed) – (first push).
- State job (816 s): inputs built; model load 93 s (ended). Part 1: `parent` 177 s, `S` 60 s, `SB` 61 s, `Q` 60 s, `C` 60 s, `R` 60 s (ended). Part 2: `parent` 32 s, `S` 50 s, `SB` 48 s, `Q` 37 s, `C` 39 s, `R` 37 s (ended).
- Known-answer test (2 s). Part 1 analysis (99 s); replication < 1 s. Part 2 analysis.
- Pushes: (pre-flight) (stop check, known-answer test) (Part 1) (Part 2), then README and final push.
- GPU busy about 14 minutes (about 0.23 GPU-hours) for R05; addendum A (about 0.36 GPU-hours; `addendum_a/README.md`). No API spend.

## Files

On the branch (`e05/r05/`):
- `results/part1_cells.jsonl`: every Part 1 cell (model × P01/P02/P05 × trigger × block 8/16/24/32, `with_sys`), all measures with intervals, M6 on five directions (blocks 24/32), M7 for both remainders.
- `results/part1_per_history.jsonl`: per-history scalars (`‖g_m‖`, `‖g_p‖`, `⟨g_m, g_p⟩`, `‖s‖`, `cos(g_m, g_p)`, `cos(s, −g_p)`, `‖σ‖`, `‖η‖` for parent and model, `‖d‖`) and, at blocks 24/32, per-history projections of `g_m`, `g_p`, `σ`, `η`, `d` on the five directions.
- `results/part1_projections.jsonl`: per-prompt projections of every saved state (6 models × 700 prompts) on the five directions at blocks 24 and 32.
- `results/part2_summary.jsonl` ((a)–(f) per model and block), `results/part2_per_persona.jsonl` (per model × block × persona: α_r, ρ_r, E1_r with intervals, `x_r`, per-direction parent gap, model gap, observed, predicted, remainder; mean axis projections).
- `results/replication.json`, `results/known_answer_test.json`, `results/tables.md` (all tables), `results/tables.json`.
- `push_states/part1_states_b32_<model>.pt` (float16 [700, 5120], rows as `push_states/part1_index.json`); `push_states/part2_mean_states_b32_<model>.pt` (float16 [271, 5120], mean over the 20 scripts; rows as `push_states/part2_mean_states_index.json`).
- `driver/` (code: `r05_states.py`, `r05_lib.py`, `r05_analysis.py`, `r05_report.py`, `r05_readme.py`, `push.sh`), `logs/` (events, input checks with the 271 system prompts and example renderings, stop check, hashes).
